1	import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'
2	import { randomBytes } from 'crypto'
3	import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
4	import { isAdminRequest } from '@/lib/admin-session'
5	
6	export const runtime = 'nodejs'
7	export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'
8	
9	const UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i
10	
11	// Friendly alphabet: no 0/O, no 1/I/L — easy to read aloud over the phone.
12	const CODE_ALPHABET = 'REDACTED'
13	
14	function generateActivationCode(): string {
15	  const bytes = randomBytes(8)
16	  let out = ''
17	  for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) out += CODE_ALPHABET[bytes[i] % CODE_ALPHABET.length]
18	  return `YFF-${out}`
19	}
20	
21	export async function POST(req: NextRequest, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
22	  if (!isAdminRequest(req)) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Admin login required.' }, { status: 401 })
23	
24	  const { id } = await params
25	  if (!UUID_RE.test(id)) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid rider id.' }, { status: 400 })
26	
27	  const supabase = createClient(
28	    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
29	    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,
30	  )
31	
32	  const { data: rider } = await supabase
33	    .from('delivery_boys')
34	    .select('id, status, activation_code')
35	    .eq('id', id)
36	    .maybeSingle()
37	
38	  if (!rider) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rider not found.' }, { status: 404 })
39	  if (rider.status === 'active') {
40	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rider is already active.' }, { status: 409 })
41	  }
42	  if (rider.status === 'suspended') {
43	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rider is suspended. Reinstate first.' }, { status: 409 })
44	  }
45	
46	  // If a prior approval issued a code that hasn't been consumed, reuse it
47	  // so the admin doesn't have to call the rider again with a new one.
48	  const code = rider.activation_code || generateActivationCode()
49	
50	  const { error: updErr } = await supabase
51	    .from('delivery_boys')
52	    .update({
53	      status: 'approved',
54	      activation_code: code,
55	      approved_at: new Date().toISOString(),
56	    })
57	    .eq('id', id)
58	
59	  if (updErr) {
60	    console.error('[YFF admin/approve] update failed:', updErr.message)
61	    return NextResponse.json({ error: updErr.message }, { status: 500 })
62	  }
63	
64	  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, activation_code: code })
65	}
66	
```

> AGENT

Here's how the delivery boy (rider) activation code works:

**Flow:**
1. Rider applies via `/rider/signup` — no code yet, status `pending_approval`.
2. Owner approves them from the admin panel. The server generates the code (`src/app/api/admin/riders/[id]/approve/route.ts:48`).
3. Owner reads the code to the rider over the phone.
4. Rider enters it at `/rider/activate` to unlock their account.

**Code format:** `YFF-XXXXXXXX` — prefix `YFF-` plus 8 random characters from a friendly alphabet that excludes look-alikes:

```
REDACTED
```

No `0`/`O`, no `1`/`I`/`L`, so it's easy to read aloud over the phone. Example: `YFF-K3M9PQRT`.

**Other notes:**
- Codes are case-insensitive — `/rider/activate` route uppercases input before comparing (`src/app/api/rider/activate/route.ts:27,67`).
- If a rider is approved but hasn't activated yet, re-approval reuses the same code (`approve/route.ts:48`) so the owner doesn't have to call again.
- After successful activation the code is cleared from the DB (`activate/route.ts:76`), so it's single-use.
- The rider must already have an account (phone + password) from `/rider/signup` before the code is useful — the code only flips status from `approved` → `active`.

> AGENT

**"Owner" = you** — the person running YourFamilyFarmer who logs into the admin panel at `/admin/login`.

In the code, "owner" is just the user-facing word for whoever has admin access. It's the same person who:
- Approves new rider applications (`/admin` → rider list → Approve button, which calls `api/admin/riders/[id]/approve`)
- Gets the generated `YFF-XXXXXXXX` code back and reads it to the rider over the phone
- Can suspend/reinstate riders
- Manages farmer joins, orders, etc.

There's only one admin role in the app (gated by `isAdminRequest` / `admin-session.ts`) — riders see it as "the owner" in UI copy because it's friendlier than "admin" for a delivery boy who may not be familiar with the term.

So in practice: rider signs up → **you** open `/admin`, click approve, the system shows the code on your screen → you call the rider and tell them the code.

182	            className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-base focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none"
183	            required

214	            required
215	          />
216	        </div>
217	
218	        <div>

221	          </label>

229	            required
230	          />
231	        </div>

234	          <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-700 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-1">
235	            ID proof photo (Aadhaar / DL) / గుర్తింపు కార్డు
236	          </label>
237	          {preview ? (
238	            <div className="space-y-2">
239	              {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
240	              <img src={preview} alt="ID proof" className="w-full max-h-56 object-contain rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50" />
241	              <label className="text-xs font-bold text-blue-700 underline cursor-pointer">
242	                Change photo

244	              </label>
245	            </div>

248	              📎 Attach ID photo / గుర్తింపు పత్రం
249	              <input type="file" accept="image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp" className="hidden" onChange={handlePickFile} />
250	            </label>
251	          )}
252	        </div>
253	
254	        {error && (
255	          <p className="text-xs text-red-700 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 font-semibold">
256	            {error}
257	          </p>
258	        )}
259	
260	        <button
261	          type="submit"
262	          disabled={submitting}
263	          className="w-full bg-green-700 text-white font-bold py-4 rounded-xl text-base active:bg-green-800 disabled:opacity-50"

266	        </button>

278	  )

1	import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'
2	import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
3	import { hashPassword } from '@/lib/password'
4	import { normalizePhone } from '@/lib/phone'
5	import { rateLimit } from '@/lib/rate-limit'
6	
7	export const runtime = 'nodejs'
8	export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'
9	
10	const ALLOWED_TYPES = new Set(['image/jpeg', 'image/jpg', 'image/png', 'image/webp'])
11	const MAX_BYTES = 8 * 1024 * 1024 // 8MB — ID photos are usually a single page
12	const VEHICLE_TYPES = new Set(['bike', 'scooter', 'cycle', 'auto', 'other'])
13	
14	function bad(msg: string, status = 400) {
15	  return NextResponse.json({ error: msg }, { status })
16	}
17	
18	export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
19	  const ip = req.headers.get('x-forwarded-for')?.split(',')[0]?.trim() || 'unknown'
20	  if (!rateLimit(`rider-reg:${ip}`, 5, 60 * 60 * 1000)) {
21	    return NextResponse.json(
22	      { error: 'Too many sign-up attempts. Try again in an hour.' },
23	      { status: 429 },
24	    )
25	  }
26	
27	  let form: FormData
28	  try {
29	    form = await req.formData()
30	  } catch {
31	    return bad('Invalid upload payload.')
32	  }
33	
34	  const name = String(form.get('name') ?? '').trim().slice(0, 80)
35	  const phone = normalizePhone(form.get('phone') as string | null)
36	  const altPhone = normalizePhone(form.get('alt_phone') as string | null)
37	  const password = String(form.get('password') ?? '')
38	  const vehicleType = String(form.get('vehicle_type') ?? '').trim().toLowerCase()
39	  const vehicleNumber = String(form.get('vehicle_number') ?? '').trim().toUpperCase().slice(0, 20)
40	  const serviceAreas = String(form.get('service_areas') ?? '').trim().slice(0, 400)
41	  const file = form.get('file')
42	
43	  if (!name) return bad('Please enter your name.')
44	  if (!phone) return bad('Enter a valid 10-digit phone number.')
45	  if (password.length < 6) return bad('Password must be at least 6 characters.')
46	  if (password.length > 128) return bad('Password is too long.')
47	  if (!VEHICLE_TYPES.has(vehicleType)) return bad('Pick a vehicle type.')
48	  if (!vehicleNumber) return bad('Enter your vehicle number.')
49	  if (!serviceAreas) return bad('Enter the areas you can deliver to.')
50	  if (!(file instanceof File)) return bad('Attach a photo of your ID proof.')
51	  if (!ALLOWED_TYPES.has(file.type)) return bad('Only JPG, PNG, or WEBP images are allowed.')
52	  if (file.size === 0) return bad('ID photo is empty.')
53	  if (file.size > MAX_BYTES) return bad('ID photo is too large. Max 8MB.')
54	
55	  const supabase = createClient(
56	    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
57	    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,
58	  )
59	
60	  console.log('[YFF rider-register] attempt phone=%s name=%s', phone, name)
61	
62	  const { data: existing, error: lookupErr } = await supabase
63	    .from('delivery_boys')
64	    .select('id, status')
65	    .eq('phone', phone)
66	    .maybeSingle()
67	
68	  if (lookupErr) {
69	    console.error('[YFF rider-register] lookup failed:', lookupErr.code, lookupErr.message)
70	    if (lookupErr.message?.includes('does not exist') || lookupErr.code === '42P01') {
71	      return bad('delivery_boys table is missing. Run scripts/delivery-feature-migration.sql first.', 500)
72	    }
73	    return bad(`Database error: ${lookupErr.message}`, 500)
74	  }
75	  if (existing) {
76	    return bad('An application already exists for this phone. Use the same number to log in once activated.', 409)
77	  }
78	
79	  const ext = file.type === 'image/png' ? 'png' : file.type === 'image/webp' ? 'webp' : 'jpg'
80	  const path = `${phone}-${Date.now()}.${ext}`
81	
82	  const { error: upErr } = await supabase.storage
83	    .from('rider-id-proofs')
84	    .upload(path, file, { contentType: file.type, upsert: false })
85	
86	  if (upErr) {
87	    console.error('[YFF rider-register] id-proof upload failed:', upErr.message)
88	    return bad('Could not save ID photo. Please try again.', 500)
89	  }
90	
91	  const password_hash = hashPassword(password)
92	  const { data: created, error: insertErr } = await supabase
93	    .from('delivery_boys')
94	    .insert({
95	      name,
96	      phone,
97	      alt_phone: altPhone || null,
98	      password_hash,
99	      vehicle_type: vehicleType,
100	      vehicle_number: vehicleNumber,
101	      id_proof_path: path,
102	      service_areas: serviceAreas,
103	      status: 'pending_approval',
104	    })
105	    .select('id')
106	    .single()
107	
108	  if (insertErr || !created) {
109	    console.error('[YFF rider-register] insert failed:', insertErr?.code, insertErr?.message)
110	    // Best-effort: drop the just-uploaded photo so we don't leak orphan storage objects
111	    await supabase.storage.from('rider-id-proofs').remove([path]).catch(() => null)
112	    return bad(insertErr?.message || 'Could not save your application. Please try again.', 500)
113	  }
114	
115	  console.log('[YFF rider-register] success id=%s', created.id)
116	  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, status: 'pending_approval' })
117	}
118

1	import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'
2	import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
3	import { verifyPassword } from '@/lib/password'
4	import { normalizePhone } from '@/lib/phone'
5	import { setRiderSessionCookie } from '@/lib/rider-session'
6	import { rateLimit } from '@/lib/rate-limit'
7	
8	export const runtime = 'nodejs'
9	export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'
10	
11	function bad(msg: string, status = 400) {
12	  return NextResponse.json({ error: msg }, { status })
13	}
14	
15	export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
16	  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null)
17	  if (!body || typeof body !== 'object') return bad('Invalid request.')
18	
19	  const phone = normalizePhone(body.phone)
20	  const password = String(body.password ?? '')
21	
22	  if (!phone) return bad('Enter a valid 10-digit phone number.')
23	  if (!password) return bad('Enter your password.')
24	
25	  const ip = req.headers.get('x-forwarded-for')?.split(',')[0]?.trim() || 'unknown'
26	  if (
27	    !rateLimit(`rider-login:phone:${phone}`, 5, 10 * 60 * 1000) ||
28	    !rateLimit(`rider-login:ip:${ip}`, 30, 10 * 60 * 1000)
29	  ) {
30	    return bad('Too many login attempts. Please try again in a few minutes.', 429)
31	  }
32	
33	  const supabase = createClient(
34	    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
35	    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,
36	  )
37	
38	  const { data: rider, error } = await supabase
39	    .from('delivery_boys')
40	    .select('id, name, phone, status, password_hash')
41	    .eq('phone', phone)
42	    .maybeSingle()
43	
44	  if (error) {
45	    console.error('[YFF rider-login] lookup failed:', error.code, error.message)
46	    if (error.message?.includes('does not exist') || error.code === '42P01') {
47	      return bad('delivery_boys table is missing. Run scripts/delivery-feature-migration.sql first.', 500)
48	    }
49	    return bad(`Database error: ${error.message}`, 500)
50	  }
51	  if (!rider) return bad('No rider account for this number. Sign up first.', 404)
52	
53	  if (!rider.password_hash || !verifyPassword(password, rider.password_hash)) {
54	    return bad('Wrong password.', 401)
55	  }
56	
57	  // Lifecycle gate — only fully activated accounts can hold a session.
58	  if (rider.status === 'pending_approval') {
59	    return bad('Your application is waiting for owner approval.', 403)
60	  }
61	  if (rider.status === 'approved') {
62	    return bad('Your account is approved. Please activate it with the code from the owner first.', 403)
63	  }
64	  if (rider.status === 'suspended') {
65	    return bad('Your account is suspended. Contact the owner.', 403)
66	  }
67	  if (rider.status !== 'active') {
68	    return bad('Your account is not active.', 403)
69	  }
70	
71	  await supabase
72	    .from('delivery_boys')
73	    .update({ last_login_at: new Date().toISOString() })
74	    .eq('id', rider.id)
75	
76	  console.log('[YFF rider-login] success id=%s', rider.id)
77	
78	  const res = NextResponse.json({
79	    ok: true,
80	    rider: { id: rider.id, name: rider.name, phone: rider.phone },
81	  })
82	  try {
83	    setRiderSessionCookie(res, rider.id)
84	  } catch (e) {
85	    const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Session setup failed.'
86	    return bad(msg, 500)
87	  }
88	  return res
89	}
90

```
1	import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'
2	import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
3	import { getRiderSessionFromRequest } from '@/lib/rider-session'
4	
5	export const runtime = 'nodejs'
6	export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'
7	
8	type DeliveryStatus = 'unassigned' | 'assigned' | 'picked_up' | 'out_for_delivery' | 'delivered'
9	
10	type OrderRow = {
11	  id: string
12	  farmer_id: string
13	  produce_name: string | null
14	  quantity: number | null
15	  unit: string | null
16	  total_price: number | null
17	  buyer_name: string | null
18	  buyer_phone: string | null
19	  payment_method: string | null
20	  payment_status: string | null
21	  delivery_status: DeliveryStatus | null
22	  delivery_address: string | null
23	  delivery_landmark: string | null
24	  delivery_pincode: string | null
25	  delivery_alt_phone: string | null
26	  delivery_boy_id: string | null
27	  assigned_at: string | null
28	  picked_up_at: string | null
29	  out_for_delivery_at: string | null
30	  created_at: string
31	}
32	
33	type Farmer = { id: string; name: string; village: string; phone: string | null }
34	
35	export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
36	  const session = getRiderSessionFromRequest(req)
37	  if (!session) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Not logged in.' }, { status: 401 })
38	
39	  const supabase = createClient(
40	    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
41	    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,
42	  )
43	
44	  // Gate: only active riders see orders. Suspended/pending accounts are kicked
45	  // out of /api/rider/me as well, but we double-check here to be safe.
46	  const { data: rider } = await supabase
47	    .from('delivery_boys')
48	    .select('id, status')
49	    .eq('id', session.riderId)
50	    .maybeSingle()
51	
52	  if (!rider || rider.status !== 'active') {
53	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Account not active.' }, { status: 403 })
54	  }
55	
56	  // Available — farmer-approved home deliveries that no rider has taken yet.
57	  // Mine — anything currently assigned to me and not yet delivered.
58	  const { data: availableRaw, error: availErr } = await supabase
59	    .from('orders')
60	    .select(
61	      'id, farmer_id, produce_name, quantity, unit, total_price, payment_method, payment_status, delivery_pincode, delivery_status, created_at',
62	    )
63	    .eq('delivery_type', 'home_delivery')
64	    .eq('status', 'approved')
65	    .is('delivery_boy_id', null)
66	    .or('delivery_status.is.null,delivery_status.eq.unassigned')
67	    .order('created_at', { ascending: true })
68	    .limit(50) as { data: OrderRow[] | null; error: { message: string } | null }
69	
70	  if (availErr) {
71	    console.error('[YFF rider/orders] available query failed:', availErr.message)
72	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not load orders.' }, { status: 500 })
73	  }
74	
75	  const { data: mineRaw, error: mineErr } = await supabase
76	    .from('orders')
77	    .select(
78	      'id, farmer_id, produce_name, quantity, unit, total_price, buyer_name, buyer_phone, payment_method, payment_status, delivery_status, delivery_address, delivery_landmark, delivery_pincode, delivery_alt_phone, delivery_boy_id, assigned_at, picked_up_at, out_for_delivery_at, created_at',
79	    )
80	    .eq('delivery_boy_id', session.riderId)
81	    .in('delivery_status', ['assigned', 'picked_up', 'out_for_delivery'])
82	    .order('assigned_at', { ascending: true })
83	    .limit(50) as { data: OrderRow[] | null; error: { message: string } | null }
84	
85	  if (mineErr) {
86	    console.error('[YFF rider/orders] mine query failed:', mineErr.message)
87	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not load your deliveries.' }, { status: 500 })
88	  }
89	
90	  const farmerIds = [...new Set([
91	    ...(availableRaw ?? []).map((o) => o.farmer_id),
92	    ...(mineRaw ?? []).map((o) => o.farmer_id),
93	  ].filter(Boolean))]
94	
95	  let farmerMap: Record<string, Farmer> = {}
96	  if (farmerIds.length > 0) {
97	    const { data: farmers } = await supabase
98	      .from('farmers')
99	      .select('id, name, village, phone')
100	      .in('id', farmerIds)
101	    farmerMap = Object.fromEntries(
102	      (farmers ?? []).map((f) => [f.id, { id: f.id, name: f.name, village: f.village, phone: f.phone ?? null }]),
103	    )
104	  }
105	
106	  // Available rows are public to all active riders — withhold consumer name,
107	  // phone, and street so a rider can't fish for personal info by browsing.
108	  const available = (availableRaw ?? []).map((o) => {
109	    const farmer = farmerMap[o.farmer_id] ?? null
110	    return {
111	      id: o.id,
112	      produce_name: o.produce_name,
113	      quantity: o.quantity,
114	      unit: o.unit,
115	      total_price: o.total_price,
116	      payment_method: o.payment_method,
117	      payment_status: o.payment_status,
118	      delivery_pincode: o.delivery_pincode,
119	      created_at: o.created_at,
120	      farmer: farmer ? { name: farmer.name, village: farmer.village } : null,
121	    }
122	  })
123	
124	  const mine = (mineRaw ?? []).map((o) => ({
125	    ...o,
126	    farmer: farmerMap[o.farmer_id] ?? null,
127	  }))
128	
129	  return NextResponse.json({ available, mine })
130	}
131	
```

1	-- ================================================================
2	-- YFF Feature Migration
3	-- Run in: Supabase Dashboard → SQL Editor → New Query → Run
4	-- ================================================================
5	
6	-- 1. Harvest date on produce listings
7	ALTER TABLE produce_listings
8	  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS harvest_date date;
9	
10	-- 2. Cover photo for farmer profile banner
11	ALTER TABLE farmers
12	  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS cover_photo_url text;
13	
14	-- 3. Farmer avatar/profile photo
15	ALTER TABLE farmers
16	  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS photo_url text;
17	
18	-- 4. Pesticide test certificate (photo URL)
19	ALTER TABLE farmers
20	  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS pesticide_cert_url text;
21	
22	-- 5. Pickup schedule: {days: string[], time_from: string, time_to: string}
23	ALTER TABLE farmers
24	  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS pickup_slots jsonb;
25	
26	-- 6. OTP table for secure farmer login
27	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS farmer_otps (
28	  id          uuid        DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() PRIMARY KEY,
29	  phone       text        NOT NULL,
30	  otp         text        NOT NULL,
31	  expires_at  timestamptz NOT NULL,
32	  used        boolean     DEFAULT false,
33	  created_at  timestamptz DEFAULT now()
34	);
35	
36	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_farmer_otps_phone   ON farmer_otps(phone);
37	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_farmer_otps_expires ON farmer_otps(expires_at);
38	
39	-- RLS: only service-role key (used in API routes) can access
40	ALTER TABLE farmer_otps ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
41	
42	-- 7. Payment method and status on orders (COD support)
43	ALTER TABLE orders ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS payment_method text DEFAULT 'cod';
44	ALTER TABLE orders ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS payment_status text DEFAULT 'pending';
45	
46	-- 8. Farmer GPS location for distance-based discovery
47	ALTER TABLE farmers ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS lat numeric(10,7);
48	ALTER TABLE farmers ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS lng numeric(10,7);
49	ALTER TABLE farmers ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS location_name text;
50	
51	-- 9. Password hash for farmer login
52	ALTER TABLE farmers ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS password_hash text;
53	
54	-- 10. Farm gallery photos
55	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS media (
56	  id          uuid        DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() PRIMARY KEY,
57	  farmer_id   uuid        REFERENCES farmers(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
58	  type        text        NOT NULL DEFAULT 'photo',
59	  url         text        NOT NULL,
60	  caption     text,
61	  sort_order  integer     DEFAULT 0,
62	  created_at  timestamptz DEFAULT now()
63	);
64	
65	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_media_farmer_id ON media(farmer_id);
66	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_media_type      ON media(type);
67	
68	-- 11. Reviewer phone for duplicate-review prevention
69	ALTER TABLE reviews ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS reviewer_phone text;
70	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_reviews_farmer_phone ON reviews(farmer_id, reviewer_phone);
71	
72	-- 12. WhatsApp click analytics (insert-only, no auth needed)
73	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS wa_clicks (
74	  id         uuid        DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() PRIMARY KEY,
75	  farmer_id  uuid        REFERENCES farmers(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
76	  created_at timestamptz DEFAULT now()
77	);
78	
79	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_wa_clicks_farmer ON wa_clicks(farmer_id);
80	
81	-- Allow anonymous inserts for wa_clicks (consumer clicks)
82	ALTER TABLE wa_clicks ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
83	DO $$
84	BEGIN
85	  IF NOT EXISTS (
86	    SELECT 1 FROM pg_policies
87	    WHERE tablename = 'wa_clicks' AND policyname = 'anon insert wa_clicks'
88	  ) THEN
89	    EXECUTE 'CREATE POLICY "anon insert wa_clicks" ON wa_clicks FOR INSERT TO anon WITH CHECK (true)';
90	  END IF;
91	END $$;
92

44	export default function RiderDashboardPage() {
45	  const router = useRouter()
46	  const [rider, setRider] = useState<Rider | null>(null)
47	  const [available, setAvailable] = useState<AvailableOrder[]>([])
48	  const [mine, setMine] = useState<MyOrder[]>([])
49	  const [tab, setTab] = useState<'available' | 'mine'>('available')

53	  const [otpInputs, setOtpInputs] = useState<Record<string, string>>({})
54	  const [otpError, setOtpError] = useState<Record<string, string>>({})
55	
56	  // Pull current rider once. If the cookie is invalid or the account isn't
57	  // active, /api/rider/me returns rider:null and we kick back to login.
58	  useEffect(() => {
59	    let cancelled = false
60	    fetch('/api/rider/me', { credentials: 'same-origin' })
61	      .then((r) => r.json())
62	      .then((json) => {
63	        if (cancelled) return
64	        if (!json?.rider) { router.replace('/rider/login'); return }
65	        setRider(json.rider as Rider)
66	      })
67	      .catch(() => { if (!cancelled) router.replace('/rider/login') })
68	    return () => { cancelled = true }
69	  }, [router])
70	
71	  const refresh = useCallback(async () => {
72	    setError('')
73	    const r = await fetch('/api/rider/orders', { credentials: 'same-origin' }).catch(() => null)
74	    if (!r) { setError('Network error. / నెట్‌వర్క్ లోపం'); setLoading(false); return }
75	    const json = await r.json().catch(() => ({}))

77	    setAvailable((json.available ?? []) as AvailableOrder[])
78	    setMine((json.mine ?? []) as MyOrder[])
79	    setLoading(false)
80	  }, [])

  "old_string": "import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'\nimport { randomBytes } from 'crypto'\nimport { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\nimport { isAdminRequest } from '@/lib/admin-session'\n\nexport const runtime = 'nodejs'\nexport const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'\n\nconst UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i\n\n// Friendly alphabet: no 0/O, no 1/I/L — easy to read aloud over the phone.\nconst CODE_ALPHABET = 'REDACTED'\n\nfunction generateActivationCode(): string {\n  const bytes = randomBytes(8)\n  let out = ''\n  for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) out += CODE_ALPHABET[bytes[i] % CODE_ALPHABET.length]\n  return `YFF-${out}`\n}\n\nexport async function POST(req: NextRequest, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {\n  if (!isAdminRequest(req)) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Admin login required.' }, { status: 401 })\n\n  const { id } = await params\n  if (!UUID_RE.test(id)) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid rider id.' }, { status: 400 })\n\n  const supabase = createClient(\n    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,\n    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,\n  )\n\n  const { data: rider } = await supabase\n    .from('delivery_boys')\n    .select('id, status, activation_code')\n    .eq('id', id)\n    .maybeSingle()\n\n  if (!rider) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rider not found.' }, { status: 404 })\n  if (rider.status === 'active') {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rider is already active.' }, { status: 409 })\n  }\n  if (rider.status === 'suspended') {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rider is suspended. Reinstate first.' }, { status: 409 })\n  }\n\n  // If a prior approval issued a code that hasn't been consumed, reuse it\n  // so the admin doesn't have to call the rider again with a new one.\n  const code = rider.activation_code || generateActivationCode()\n\n  const { error: updErr } = await supabase\n    .from('delivery_boys')\n    .update({\n      status: 'approved',\n      activation_code: code,\n      approved_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n    })\n    .eq('id', id)\n\n  if (updErr) {\n    console.error('[YFF admin/approve] update failed:', updErr.message)\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: updErr.message }, { status: 500 })\n  }\n\n  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, activation_code: code })\n}",
  "new_string": "import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'\nimport { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'\nimport { isAdminRequest } from '@/lib/admin-session'\n\nexport const runtime = 'nodejs'\nexport const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'\n\nconst UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i\n\nexport async function POST(req: NextRequest, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {\n  if (!isAdminRequest(req)) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Admin login required.' }, { status: 401 })\n\n  const { id } = await params\n  if (!UUID_RE.test(id)) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid rider id.' }, { status: 400 })\n\n  const supabase = createClient(\n    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,\n    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,\n  )\n\n  const { data: rider } = await supabase\n    .from('delivery_boys')\n    .select('id, status')\n    .eq('id', id)\n    .maybeSingle()\n\n  if (!rider) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rider not found.' }, { status: 404 })\n  if (rider.status === 'active') {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rider is already active.' }, { status: 409 })\n  }\n  if (rider.status === 'suspended') {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Rider is suspended. Reinstate first.' }, { status: 409 })\n  }\n\n  const now = new Date().toISOString()\n  const { error: updErr } = await supabase\n    .from('delivery_boys')\n    .update({\n      status: 'active',\n      activation_code: null,\n      approved_at: now,\n      activated_at: now,\n    })\n    .eq('id', id)\n\n  if (updErr) {\n    console.error('[YFF admin/approve] update failed:', updErr.message)\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: updErr.message }, { status: 500 })\n  }\n\n  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true })\n}"
}

327	function MyOrderCard({
328	  order,

334	  onOutForDelivery,

337	  order: MyOrder

340	  onOtpChange: (v: string) => void
341	  otpError?: string
342	  onPickup: () => void
343	  onOutForDelivery: () => void
344	  onDeliver: () => void
345	}) {
346	  const isCod = order.payment_method === 'cod'
347	  const statusLabel =
348	    order.delivery_status === 'assigned' ? 'Go pick up / పికప్ చేయండి'
349	      : order.delivery_status === 'picked_up' ? 'Picked up / తీసుకున్నారు'
350	      : 'Out for delivery / డెలివరీలో'
351	  const statusColor =
352	    order.delivery_status === 'assigned' ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800'
353	      : order.delivery_status === 'picked_up' ? 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-800'
354	      : 'bg-purple-100 text-purple-800'
355	
356	  return (
357	    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 overflow-hidden">
358	      <div className="p-4 space-y-3">
359	        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
360	          <div className="min-w-0">
361	            <p className="font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-sm leading-tight">
362	              {order.produce_name || 'Order'}
363	            </p>
364	            <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5">
365	              {order.quantity ?? 0} {order.unit || 'kg'}
366	              {order.total_price ? ` · ₹${order.total_price}` : ''}
367	            </p>
368	          </div>
369	          <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ${statusColor} whitespace-nowrap`}>
370	            {statusLabel}
371	          </span>
372	        </div>
373	
374	        {isCod && (
375	          <div className="bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs">
376	            <span className="font-bold text-amber-900">💵 Collect ₹{order.total_price ?? 0}</span> in cash from the customer.<br />
377	            <span className="text-amber-800">కస్టమర్ నుండి నగదు తీసుకోండి.</span>
378	          </div>
379	        )}
380	
381	        {/* Pickup details */}
382	        {order.farmer && (

384	            <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-green-800 uppercase tracking-wide">Pickup from / పికప్</p>
385	            <p className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900">{order.farmer.name}</p>
386	            <p className="text-xs text-gray-600">{order.farmer.village}</p>
387	            {order.farmer.phone && (
388	              <a
389	                href={`tel:${order.farmer.phone}`}
390	                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 mt-1 text-xs font-bold text-green-700 underline"
391	              >

393	              </a>
394	            )}
395	          </div>
396	        )}
397	
398	        {/* Drop details */}
399	        <div className="border border-blue-100 bg-blue-50 rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 space-y-1">
400	          <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-blue-800 uppercase tracking-wide">Deliver to / డెలివరీ</p>
401	          <p className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900">{order.buyer_name || 'Customer'}</p>
402	          {order.delivery_address && (

404	          )}

Typecheck is clean. Everything is wired up. Here's what changed:

**Simple rider registration (no activation code):**
- `src/app/api/rider/register/route.ts` — new accounts are created with `status: 'active'` straight away.
- `src/app/api/rider/login/route.ts` — login gate now only rejects `suspended`; `pending_approval`/`approved` branches removed.
- `src/app/rider/signup/page.tsx` — success screen now says "You're all set! Log in" and links directly to `/rider/login`.
- `src/app/rider/login/page.tsx` — removed the "Got your activation code?" link.
- Deleted `src/app/rider/activate/page.tsx` and `src/app/api/rider/activate/route.ts`.
- `src/app/api/admin/riders/[id]/approve/route.ts` — for any legacy `pending_approval` records, admin's "Activate" button now flips them straight to `active` (no code generated).
- `src/app/admin/page.tsx` — removed the activation-code box and "/rider/activate" help text; the button is now just "Activate".

**Farm pickup address for delivery:**
- `scripts/farm-address-migration.sql` — new migration: `ALTER TABLE farmers ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS farm_address text;` **Run this in Supabase SQL Editor before testing.**
- `src/app/farmer/dashboard/page.tsx` — new textarea "Farm pickup address" in the profile edit form, saved to `farm_address`.
- `src/app/api/rider/orders/route.ts` — selects `farm_address` from farmers and returns it in both available and mine payloads.
- `src/app/rider/dashboard/page.tsx` — shows the farm address under the pickup section on both the available-orders preview and accepted-orders cards.

**Next step on your side:** run `scripts/farm-address-migration.sql` in Supabase, then `npm run dev` and try: sign up as a rider → land on login → log in → see the order with the farmer's pickup address.

1	import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'
2	import { randomInt } from 'crypto'
3	import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
4	import { getConsumerSessionFromRequest } from '@/lib/session'
5	import { getTierPrice } from '@/lib/pricing'
6	import { normalizePhone } from '@/lib/phone'
7	
8	export const runtime = 'nodejs'
9	export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'
10	
11	type IncomingItem = { listingId: string; qty: number }
12	
13	// 4-digit handover code, generated server-side at order placement. The
14	// customer reads it off their order page and reads it aloud to the rider at
15	// the door. crypto.randomInt avoids Math.random's predictability.
16	function generateHandoverOtp(): string {
17	  return String(randomInt(0, 10000)).padStart(4, '0')
18	}
19	
20	type ListingRow = {
21	  id: string
22	  name: string
23	  unit: string | null
24	  stock_qty: number | null
25	  farmer_id: string
26	  price_tier_1_qty: number | null
27	  price_tier_1_price: number | null
28	  price_tier_2_qty: number | null
29	  price_tier_2_price: number | null
30	  price_tier_3_price: number | null
31	}
32	
33	const UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i
34	
35	function bad(msg: string, status = 400) {
36	  return NextResponse.json({ error: msg }, { status })
37	}
38	
39	export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
40	  const session = getConsumerSessionFromRequest(req)
41	  if (!session) return bad('Please log in to place an order.', 401)
42	
43	  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null) as
44	    | {
45	        farmerId?: string
46	        paymentMethod?: string
47	        pickupLocation?: string | null
48	        pickupDay?: string | null
49	        items?: IncomingItem[]
50	        deliveryType?: string
51	        deliveryAddress?: string | null
52	        deliveryLandmark?: string | null
53	        deliveryPincode?: string | null
54	        deliveryAltPhone?: string | null
55	      }
56	    | null
57	
58	  if (!body) return bad('Invalid request body.')
59	  // pickupDay is currently UI-only (not a DB column); accept and ignore.
60	  const { farmerId, paymentMethod, pickupLocation, items } = body
61	
62	  if (!farmerId || !UUID_RE.test(farmerId)) return bad('Invalid farmer.')
63	  if (paymentMethod !== 'upi' && paymentMethod !== 'cod') return bad('Invalid payment method.')
64	  if (!Array.isArray(items) || items.length === 0) return bad('Cart is empty.')
65	  if (items.length > 50) return bad('Too many items.')
66	  for (const it of items) {
67	    if (!it || typeof it !== 'object') return bad('Invalid item.')
68	    if (!it.listingId || !UUID_RE.test(it.listingId)) return bad('Invalid listing id.')
69	    if (!Number.isFinite(it.qty) || it.qty <= 0 || it.qty > 10000) return bad('Invalid quantity.')
70	  }
71	
72	  const deliveryType = body.deliveryType === 'home_delivery' ? 'home_delivery' : 'self_pickup'
73	  let deliveryAddress: string | null = null
74	  let deliveryLandmark: string | null = null
75	  let deliveryPincode: string | null = null
76	  let deliveryAltPhone: string | null = null
77	
78	  if (deliveryType === 'home_delivery') {
79	    deliveryAddress = String(body.deliveryAddress ?? '').trim().slice(0, 400)
80	    deliveryLandmark = String(body.deliveryLandmark ?? '').trim().slice(0, 200) || null
81	    const rawPincode = String(body.deliveryPincode ?? '').trim()
82	    deliveryPincode = /^\d{6}$/.test(rawPincode) ? rawPincode : null
83	    const altPhone = normalizePhone(body.deliveryAltPhone)
84	    deliveryAltPhone = altPhone || null
85	
86	    if (!deliveryAddress) return bad('Enter your delivery address.')
87	    if (deliveryAddress.length < 10) return bad('Delivery address looks too short. Please add door no, street, and area.')
88	    if (!deliveryPincode) return bad('Enter a valid 6-digit pincode.')
89	  }
90	
91	  const supabase = createClient(
92	    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
93	    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,
94	  )
95	
96	  // Authoritative consumer profile (we never trust the client for buyer name/phone)
97	  const { data: consumer } = await supabase
98	    .from('consumers_auth')
99	    .select('id, name, phone')
100	    .eq('id', session.consumerId)
101	    .maybeSingle()
102	
103	  if (!consumer) return bad('Account not found. Please log in again.', 401)
104	
105	  // Farmer COD acceptance check
106	  const { data: farmer } = await supabase
107	    .from('farmers')
108	    .select('id, cod_enabled')
109	    .eq('id', farmerId)
110	    .maybeSingle()
111	
112	  if (!farmer) return bad('Farmer not found.', 404)
113	  if (paymentMethod === 'cod' && farmer.cod_enabled !== true) {
114	    return bad('This farmer is not accepting Cash on Delivery.')
115	  }
116	
117	  // Pull live listing rows — never trust prices from the client cart
118	  const listingIds = items.map((i) => i.listingId)
119	  const { data: listings } = await supabase
120	    .from('produce_listings')
121	    .select(
122	      'id, name, unit, stock_qty, farmer_id, price_tier_1_qty, price_tier_1_price, price_tier_2_qty, price_tier_2_price, price_tier_3_price',
123	    )
124	    .in('id', listingIds) as { data: ListingRow[] | null }
125	
126	  if (!listings || listings.length !== listingIds.length) {
127	    return bad('One or more items in your cart are no longer available.')
128	  }
129	
130	  const listingById = new Map(listings.map((l) => [l.id, l]))
131	  const rows: Array<Record<string, unknown>> = []
132	  let total = 0
133	  // One OTP for the whole batch — rider does a single handover at the door,
134	  // so all rows from this checkout share the same code.
135	  const sharedHandoverOtp = deliveryType === 'home_delivery' ? generateHandoverOtp() : null
136	
137	  for (const item of items) {
138	    const listing = listingById.get(item.listingId)
139	    if (!listing) return bad('Item missing.')
140	    if (listing.farmer_id !== farmerId) return bad('Items must belong to the same farmer.')
141	    if (listing.stock_qty != null && item.qty > listing.stock_qty) {
142	      return bad(`Only ${listing.stock_qty} ${listing.unit || 'kg'} of ${listing.name} available right now.`)
143	    }
144	
145	    const unitPrice = getTierPrice(item.qty, {
146	      priceTier1Qty: listing.price_tier_1_qty,
147	      priceTier1Price: listing.price_tier_1_price,
148	      priceTier2Qty: listing.price_tier_2_qty,
149	      priceTier2Price: listing.price_tier_2_price,
150	      priceTier3Price: listing.price_tier_3_price,
151	    })
152	
153	    const linePrice = unitPrice != null ? Math.round(unitPrice * item.qty) : null
154	    if (linePrice == null || linePrice <= 0) {
155	      return bad(`Price not set for ${listing.name}. Please ask the farmer.`)
156	    }
157	    total += linePrice
158	
159	    rows.push({
160	      farmer_id: farmerId,
161	      produce_listing_id: listing.id,
162	      produce_name: listing.name,
163	      quantity: item.qty,
164	      unit: listing.unit || 'kg',
165	      total_price: linePrice,
166	      buyer_name: consumer.name || 'Buyer',
167	      buyer_phone: consumer.phone,
168	      consumer_id: consumer.id,
169	      pickup_location: typeof pickupLocation === 'string' ? pickupLocation.slice(0, 200) : null,
170	      status: 'pending',
171	      payment_method: paymentMethod,
172	      payment_status: 'pending',
173	      delivery_type: deliveryType,
174	      delivery_status: deliveryType === 'home_delivery' ? 'unassigned' : null,
175	      delivery_address: deliveryAddress,
176	      delivery_landmark: deliveryLandmark,
177	      delivery_pincode: deliveryPincode,
178	      delivery_alt_phone: deliveryAltPhone,
179	      handover_otp: sharedHandoverOtp,
180	    })
181	  }
182	
183	  const { data: inserted, error: insertErr } = await supabase
184	    .from('orders')
185	    .insert(rows)
186	    .select('id')
187	
188	  if (insertErr || !inserted) {
189	    console.error('[YFF] place-order insert failed:', insertErr?.message)
190	    return bad('Could not place order. Please try again.', 500)
191	  }
192	
193	  return NextResponse.json({
194	    ok: true,
195	    orderIds: inserted.map((r) => r.id),
196	    total,
197	  })
198	}
199

307	    setProcessingOrderId(orderId)

309	    setPendingOrders((prev) => prev.filter((o) => o.id !== orderId))

316	    await supabase
317	      .from('orders')
318	      .update({ status: 'declined', decline_reason: reason })
319	      .eq('id', orderId)
320	    setPendingOrders((prev) => prev.filter((o) => o.id !== orderId))
321	    setProcessingOrderId(null)
322	    setDecliningOrder(null)
323	  }

326	    setProcessingPaidId(orderId)
327	    await supabase.from('orders').update({ payment_status: 'completed' }).eq('id', orderId)
328	    setPendingOrders((prev) =>
329	      prev.map((o) => o.id === orderId ? { ...o, payment_status: 'completed' } : o)
330	    )
331	    setProcessingPaidId(null)
332	  }
333	
334	  const handleUpdatePaymentStatus = async (orderId: string, status: 'completed' | 'failed' | 'pending') => {
335	    setProcessingPaidId(orderId)
336	    const update: Record<string, string> = { payment_status: status }
337	    if (status === 'completed') update.status = 'approved'
338	    await supabase.from('orders').update(update).eq('id', orderId)
339	    setPendingOrders((prev) =>
340	      prev.map((o) => o.id === orderId ? { ...o, payment_status: status, ...(status === 'completed' ? { status: 'approved' } : {}) } : o)
341	    )
342	    setProcessingPaidId(null)
343	  }

1	-- ================================================================
2	-- YFF — Delivery feature (rider accounts + home delivery on orders)
3	-- Run in: Supabase Dashboard → SQL Editor → New Query → Run
4	-- Safe to re-run.
5	-- ================================================================
6	
7	-- 1. Delivery boy (rider) accounts. Service-role only; the anon key has no
8	--    policies on this table.
9	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS delivery_boys (
10	  id              uuid         PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
11	  name            text         NOT NULL,
12	  phone           varchar(15)  UNIQUE NOT NULL,
13	  alt_phone       varchar(15),
14	  password_hash   text         NOT NULL,
15	  vehicle_type    text,
16	  vehicle_number  text,
17	  id_proof_path   text,
18	  service_areas   text,
19	  -- Lifecycle: pending_approval → approved (with activation_code issued) →
20	  -- active (after rider consumes the code). suspended = blocked from logging in.
21	  status          text         NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending_approval',
22	  activation_code text,
23	  approved_at     timestamptz,
24	  activated_at    timestamptz,
25	  last_login_at   timestamptz,
26	  created_at      timestamptz  DEFAULT now()
27	);
28	
29	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_delivery_boys_phone   ON delivery_boys(phone);
30	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_delivery_boys_status  ON delivery_boys(status);
31	
32	ALTER TABLE delivery_boys ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
33	
34	COMMENT ON TABLE delivery_boys IS
35	  'Rider accounts. Service-role only; password scrypt-hashed; activation_code is one-time and cleared once consumed.';
36	
37	-- 2. Orders — delivery type, address, rider linkage, handover OTP, timestamps.
38	--    'self_pickup' (default) preserves the existing flow; 'home_delivery'
39	--    opts in to the new rider workflow.
40	ALTER TABLE orders ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS delivery_type        text DEFAULT 'self_pickup';
41	ALTER TABLE orders ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS delivery_status      text;
42	ALTER TABLE orders ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS delivery_address     text;
43	ALTER TABLE orders ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS delivery_landmark    text;
44	ALTER TABLE orders ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS delivery_pincode     text;
45	ALTER TABLE orders ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS delivery_alt_phone   text;
46	ALTER TABLE orders ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS delivery_boy_id      uuid REFERENCES delivery_boys(id) ON DELETE SET NULL;
47	-- 4-digit handover code; generated server-side at order placement for
48	-- delivery_type='home_delivery'. Never exposed to the rider — they must
49	-- collect it from the consumer at the door.
50	ALTER TABLE orders ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS handover_otp         text;
51	ALTER TABLE orders ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS assigned_at          timestamptz;
52	ALTER TABLE orders ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS picked_up_at         timestamptz;
53	ALTER TABLE orders ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS out_for_delivery_at  timestamptz;
54	ALTER TABLE orders ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS delivered_at         timestamptz;
55	
56	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_orders_delivery_status   ON orders(delivery_status);
57	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_orders_delivery_boy      ON orders(delivery_boy_id);
58	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_orders_delivery_type     ON orders(delivery_type);
59	
60	-- 3. Private bucket for rider ID proofs. Same pattern as payment-proofs:
61	--    anon has no policies, only API routes (service role) can upload/sign.
62	INSERT INTO storage.buckets (id, name, public)
63	VALUES ('rider-id-proofs', 'rider-id-proofs', false)
64	ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING;
65

1	import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'
2	import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
3	import { getRiderSessionFromRequest } from '@/lib/rider-session'
4	
5	export const runtime = 'nodejs'
6	export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'
7	
8	const UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i
9	
10	export async function POST(req: NextRequest, { params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
11	  const { id } = await params
12	  if (!UUID_RE.test(id)) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid order id.' }, { status: 400 })
13	
14	  const session = getRiderSessionFromRequest(req)
15	  if (!session) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Not logged in.' }, { status: 401 })
16	
17	  const supabase = createClient(
18	    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
19	    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,
20	  )
21	
22	  const { data: rider } = await supabase
23	    .from('delivery_boys')
24	    .select('id, status')
25	    .eq('id', session.riderId)
26	    .maybeSingle()
27	  if (!rider || rider.status !== 'active') {
28	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Account not active.' }, { status: 403 })
29	  }
30	
31	  // Optimistic-lock claim — only succeeds if the order is still unclaimed and
32	  // home-delivery + approved. Two riders tapping Accept simultaneously can't
33	  // both win because the WHERE clause excludes any row that already has a
34	  // delivery_boy_id.
35	  const now = new Date().toISOString()
36	  const { data: claimed, error } = await supabase
37	    .from('orders')
38	    .update({
39	      delivery_boy_id: session.riderId,
40	      delivery_status: 'assigned',
41	      assigned_at: now,
42	    })
43	    .eq('id', id)
44	    .eq('delivery_type', 'home_delivery')
45	    .eq('status', 'approved')
46	    .is('delivery_boy_id', null)
47	    .select('id')
48	
49	  if (error) {
50	    console.error('[YFF rider/accept] update failed:', error.message)
51	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not accept order.' }, { status: 500 })
52	  }
53	  if (!claimed || claimed.length === 0) {
54	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'This order was already taken or is no longer available.' }, { status: 409 })
55	  }
56	
57	  // Belt-and-braces: if delivery_status was 'unassigned' rather than NULL,
58	  // the .is() check above wouldn't have filtered it out. We additionally
59	  // refuse if a prior status was further along the pipeline. (The combination
60	  // of the IS NULL filter on delivery_boy_id + the status checks above already
61	  // makes this safe in practice.)
62	
63	  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true })
64	}
65	
```

1	import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'
2	import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
3	import { getRiderSessionFromRequest, clearRiderSessionCookie } from '@/lib/rider-session'
4	
5	export const runtime = 'nodejs'
6	export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'
7	
8	export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
9	  const session = getRiderSessionFromRequest(req)
10	  if (!session) return NextResponse.json({ rider: null }, { status: 200 })
11	
12	  const supabase = createClient(
13	    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
14	    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,
15	  )
16	
17	  const { data: rider } = await supabase
18	    .from('delivery_boys')
19	    .select('id, name, phone, status, vehicle_type, vehicle_number')
20	    .eq('id', session.riderId)
21	    .maybeSingle()
22	
23	  // Cookie references a deleted or deactivated account — clear it so the
24	  // client treats us as logged out.
25	  if (!rider || rider.status !== 'active') {
26	    const res = NextResponse.json({ rider: null }, { status: 200 })
27	    clearRiderSessionCookie(res)
28	    return res
29	  }
30	
31	  return NextResponse.json({ rider })
32	}
33

382	  // Server-authoritative order placement. Prices, buyer identity, and COD
383	  // permission are computed/checked on the server using the consumer's
384	  // session cookie — never trusted from the cart's client-side state.
385	  const placeOrderViaApi = async (
386	    group: CartItem[],
387	    paymentMethod: 'upi' | 'cod',
388	  ): Promise<{ ok: true; orderIds: string[]; total: number } | { ok: false; error: string }> => {
389	    const f = group[0]
390	    const r = await fetch('/api/orders/place', {
391	      method: 'POST',
392	      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
393	      credentials: 'same-origin',
394	      body: JSON.stringify({
395	        farmerId: f.farmerId,
396	        paymentMethod,

> AGENT

Not a bug — the rider feed is empty because the prerequisite hasn't happened yet. Here's how I verified end-to-end:

**Why "Available (0)" right now**

The rider's "Available" feed (`src/app/api/rider/orders/route.ts:58`) filters with:
```
delivery_type = 'home_delivery'
AND status = 'approved'
AND delivery_boy_id IS NULL
AND (delivery_status IS NULL OR delivery_status = 'unassigned')
```

An order only matches all four when:
1. A consumer chose **Home Delivery** in the cart (`src/components/consumer/Cart.tsx:919-945` — defaults to Self Pickup, you have to toggle it).
2. The farmer pressed **Approve** in their dashboard (`src/app/farmer/dashboard/page.tsx:306-312`). For UPI, the order also auto-approves when the farmer marks payment received (`page.tsx:337`).
3. No other rider has accepted it yet.

So if every test order so far was "Self Pickup", or any "Home Delivery" order is still pending farmer approval, the rider will correctly see 0. That is by design — a rider must never see an unapproved/unpaid order.

**To test the flow end-to-end:**
1. Run **both** SQL migrations in Supabase: `scripts/delivery-feature-migration.sql` (if not yet run) and the new `scripts/farm-address-migration.sql`.
2. Log in as a consumer → add an item to cart → in the cart, toggle to **🛵 Home Delivery** → fill address + 6-digit pincode → place order.
3. Log in as the farmer who owns that produce → press **Approve** on the pending order (for UPI orders, instead mark payment received).
4. Now log in as rider `ravi` (or refresh `/rider/dashboard`) → the order appears under "Available".

**Bug-fixes I just made on top of the previous changes:**

- `src/app/api/rider/orders/route.ts` — hardened the farmer fetch so if `farm-address-migration.sql` hasn't been run yet, the rider still gets orders (just without the pickup address) instead of silently losing farmer info.
- `src/app/rider/dashboard/page.tsx` — the empty state now explains the 3 conditions and offers a Refresh button, so a rider seeing "0 orders" understands why.

Typecheck is clean. The other paths (`/api/rider/orders/[id]/accept`, `/pickup`, `/out-for-delivery`, `/deliver`) all use the same `delivery_type='home_delivery'` + `status='approved'` guards, so the chain is internally consistent.

If you run the two migrations and still see no orders after placing a home_delivery order and approving it as the farmer, paste the row from your Supabase `orders` table (just `id`, `status`, `delivery_type`, `delivery_status`, `delivery_boy_id`) and I'll trace exactly which filter is excluding it.

> DEVELOPER

if one delivery boy approves order then there is no chance to approve the sme delivery right

> AGENT

Correct — once a rider taps **Accept**, no one else can take that order. The protection is two layers deep, so the answer holds even when two riders tap at the exact same millisecond.

**Layer 1 — atomic conditional UPDATE** (`src/app/api/rider/orders/[id]/accept/route.ts:36-48`)

```js
.update({
  delivery_boy_id: session.riderId,
  delivery_status: 'assigned',
  assigned_at: now,
})
.eq('id', id)
.eq('delivery_type', 'home_delivery')
.eq('status', 'approved')
.is('delivery_boy_id', null)   // ← only succeeds if still unclaimed
.select('id')
```

Postgres applies the WHERE clause row-locked under the hood. The first rider's UPDATE flips `delivery_boy_id` from `NULL` → their id. When the second rider's UPDATE arrives a millisecond later, the `delivery_boy_id IS NULL` predicate is now false, so 0 rows match, `claimed.length === 0`, and the API returns:

```js
return NextResponse.json(
  { error: 'This order was already taken or is no longer available.' },
  { status: 409 },
)
```

The losing rider sees a clear "already taken" error.

**Layer 2 — the Available feed filters out claimed orders** (`src/app/api/rider/orders/route.ts:58-68`)

```
.is('delivery_boy_id', null)
.or('delivery_status.is.null,delivery_status.eq.unassigned')
```

So on the next refresh, the order disappears from every other rider's "Available" tab. They won't even see it to tap on.

So the guarantees are:
- **No double-assignment**, even under a race — Postgres enforces it at the row level.
- **One rider, one order** — `delivery_boy_id` is a single uuid column on `orders`; there is no many-to-many mapping where two riders could be linked to one order.
- Even later steps (`pickup`, `out-for-delivery`, `deliver`) re-check `.eq('delivery_boy_id', session.riderId)`, so a rider can't act on someone else's order even if they have the order id.

The only way another rider can take the order is if an admin **reassigns** it explicitly via `/api/admin/orders/[id]/reassign` — that's the deliberate escape hatch when, e.g., the original rider goes offline.

> AGENT

Yes — I scanned the code and there are a few real gaps that matter for a working delivery marketplace. Ranked by impact, not by ease.

**🔥 Must-fix bugs / loopholes (do these first)**

1. **Stock is never decremented on order placement.** `src/app/api/orders/place/route.ts:141` only *checks* `stock_qty` — it never subtracts the ordered quantity. Two consumers can each order the last 5kg of tomatoes and both succeed. Fix: decrement `stock_qty` inside the same transaction as the insert (or with an optimistic update that fails if stock would go negative).

2. **Riders see deliveries from every region.** `service_areas` is collected at signup but never matched against `delivery_pincode`. A rider in Tirupati sees orders in Vijayawada. Fix: store service pincodes as an array on the rider, and filter `/api/rider/orders` to overlap with `orders.delivery_pincode`.

3. **No delivery fee on the order.** Cart copy already admits "delivery charge is collected by the owner manually for now" (`Cart.tsx:911`). Add `delivery_fee` and `rider_payout` columns on `orders`, compute at checkout based on distance/pincode, show in the cart total. Without this you can't pay riders sustainably.

**🟠 Trust + retention (next)**

4. **Order status notifications via WhatsApp/SMS.** Right now the buyer has to keep opening the app to know if their order was approved or if the rider is on the way. One Twilio "order approved / picked up / on the way" notification per state change closes the trust loop (and ties into your existing P3 WhatsApp bot plan).

5. **Buyer cancel before farmer approves.** Currently the buyer can't withdraw a pending order. Add a Cancel button on `/consumer/orders/[id]` that flips status to `cancelled` only when `status='pending'`.

6. **Review prompt after delivery.** Reviews table exists but nothing nudges the buyer. After `delivered_at` is set, push a small "Rate this order" card to the consumer orders page. 5-min job, big lift in social proof.

7. **Rider earnings dashboard.** A rider needs to see "this week: 12 deliveries, ₹540 earned, ₹0 unpaid." Without it, retention drops. Just a `/rider/dashboard/earnings` view summing `rider_payout` from completed orders.

**🟢 Growth (later)**

8. **Distance-based discovery on the consumer landing page.** You already store `lat/lng` on farmers and capture buyer location — use it to sort farmers by km, not just by region slug.

9. **Realtime/push for the rider dashboard.** Farmer dashboard already uses Supabase realtime (`page.tsx:256-271`); the rider polls. Subscribe the rider to `orders` inserts so a new home_delivery shows up without refresh.

10. **Repeat-order / favorite farmer.** A "Reorder last week's vegetables" button on the consumer home page. Cheap to build, drives recurring GMV.

**My recommendation:** ship **1, 2, 3** as one bundle — stock + service-area routing + delivery fee are the three loopholes that will bite you the moment real volume hits. **4** (WhatsApp notifications) is the natural next step since it unblocks the dormant P3 WhatsApp bot work in your memory.

Want me to start on stock decrement (#1)? That's the lowest-risk, highest-value single fix and won't touch the new rider/farmer code we just shipped.

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/scripts/must-fix-migration.sql",
  "content": "-- ================================================================\n-- YFF Must-Fix Migration\n-- 1. Atomic stock decrement RPCs (prevents overselling)\n-- 2. service_pincodes[] on delivery_boys (route deliveries by pincode)\n-- 3. delivery_fee + rider_payout on orders (pay riders sustainably)\n-- Run in: Supabase Dashboard → SQL Editor → New Query → Run\n-- Safe to re-run.\n-- ================================================================\n\n-- 1. Stock decrement / increment\n-- decrement_stock returns true only if the row had enough stock (or\n-- unlimited NULL stock). Treating the UPDATE + ROW_COUNT check as a\n-- single transactional statement is what makes this race-safe.\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION decrement_stock(p_listing_id uuid, p_qty numeric)\nRETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE plpgsql\nAS $$\nDECLARE\n  affected int;\n  has_null_stock boolean;\nBEGIN\n  UPDATE produce_listings\n  SET stock_qty = stock_qty - p_qty\n  WHERE id = p_listing_id\n    AND stock_qty IS NOT NULL\n    AND stock_qty >= p_qty;\n\n  GET DIAGNOSTICS affected = ROW_COUNT;\n  IF affected > 0 THEN\n    RETURN TRUE;\n  END IF;\n\n  -- No row updated. Either the listing is missing, stock_qty was too low,\n  -- or stock_qty is NULL (unlimited). Treat NULL stock as success.\n  SELECT (stock_qty IS NULL) INTO has_null_stock\n  FROM produce_listings WHERE id = p_listing_id;\n\n  RETURN COALESCE(has_null_stock, FALSE);\nEND;\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION increment_stock(p_listing_id uuid, p_qty numeric)\nRETURNS void\nLANGUAGE plpgsql\nAS $$\nBEGIN\n  UPDATE produce_listings\n  SET stock_qty = stock_qty + p_qty\n  WHERE id = p_listing_id AND stock_qty IS NOT NULL;\nEND;\n$$;\n\n-- 2. Rider service pincodes — 6-digit strings the rider commits to cover.\n-- An empty / NULL array means \"no preference, show everything\" so legacy\n-- accounts keep working until they edit their profile.\nALTER TABLE delivery_boys\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS service_pincodes text[];\n\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_delivery_boys_service_pincodes\n  ON delivery_boys USING gin (service_pincodes);\n\n-- 3. Delivery fee + rider payout on orders.\n-- For a multi-row batch (one cart → many produce rows) we stamp the full\n-- fee on the FIRST row only and 0 on the rest, so SUM(rider_payout) over\n-- a batch equals one fee.\nALTER TABLE orders ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS delivery_fee integer DEFAULT 0;\nALTER TABLE orders ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS rider_payout integer DEFAULT 0;\n"
}

  "old_string": "  const listingById = new Map(listings.map((l) => [l.id, l]))\n  const rows: Array<Record<string, unknown>> = []\n  let total = 0\n  // One OTP for the whole batch — rider does a single handover at the door,\n  // so all rows from this checkout share the same code.\n  const sharedHandoverOtp = deliveryType === 'home_delivery' ? generateHandoverOtp() : null\n\n  for (const item of items) {\n    const listing = listingById.get(item.listingId)\n    if (!listing) return bad('Item missing.')\n    if (listing.farmer_id !== farmerId) return bad('Items must belong to the same farmer.')\n    if (listing.stock_qty != null && item.qty > listing.stock_qty) {\n      return bad(`Only ${listing.stock_qty} ${listing.unit || 'kg'} of ${listing.name} available right now.`)\n    }\n\n    const unitPrice = getTierPrice(item.qty, {\n      priceTier1Qty: listing.price_tier_1_qty,\n      priceTier1Price: listing.price_tier_1_price,\n      priceTier2Qty: listing.price_tier_2_qty,\n      priceTier2Price: listing.price_tier_2_price,\n      priceTier3Price: listing.price_tier_3_price,\n    })\n\n    const linePrice = unitPrice != null ? Math.round(unitPrice * item.qty) : null\n    if (linePrice == null || linePrice <= 0) {\n      return bad(`Price not set for ${listing.name}. Please ask the farmer.`)\n    }\n    total += linePrice\n\n    rows.push({\n      farmer_id: farmerId,\n      produce_listing_id: listing.id,\n      produce_name: listing.name,\n      quantity: item.qty,\n      unit: listing.unit || 'kg',\n      total_price: linePrice,\n      buyer_name: consumer.name || 'Buyer',\n      buyer_phone: consumer.phone,\n      consumer_id: consumer.id,\n      pickup_location: typeof pickupLocation === 'string' ? pickupLocation.slice(0, 200) : null,\n      status: 'pending',\n      payment_method: paymentMethod,\n      payment_status: 'pending',\n      delivery_type: deliveryType,\n      delivery_status: deliveryType === 'home_delivery' ? 'unassigned' : null,\n      delivery_address: deliveryAddress,\n      delivery_landmark: deliveryLandmark,\n      delivery_pincode: deliveryPincode,\n      delivery_alt_phone: deliveryAltPhone,\n      handover_otp: sharedHandoverOtp,\n    })\n  }\n\n  const { data: inserted, error: insertErr } = await supabase\n    .from('orders')\n    .insert(rows)\n    .select('id')\n\n  if (insertErr || !inserted) {\n    console.error('[YFF] place-order insert failed:', insertErr?.message)\n    return bad('Could not place order. Please try again.', 500)\n  }\n\n  return NextResponse.json({\n    ok: true,\n    orderIds: inserted.map((r) => r.id),\n    total,\n  })\n}",
  "new_string": "  const listingById = new Map(listings.map((l) => [l.id, l]))\n  const rows: Array<Record<string, unknown>> = []\n  let total = 0\n  // One OTP for the whole batch — rider does a single handover at the door,\n  // so all rows from this checkout share the same code.\n  const sharedHandoverOtp = deliveryType === 'home_delivery' ? generateHandoverOtp() : null\n  const deliveryFee = deliveryType === 'home_delivery' ? DELIVERY_FEE_RUPEES : 0\n\n  // Validate first (price + ownership) before we touch any stock. Stock\n  // claims happen below with the RPC so two cart submits can't oversell.\n  for (const item of items) {\n    const listing = listingById.get(item.listingId)\n    if (!listing) return bad('Item missing.')\n    if (listing.farmer_id !== farmerId) return bad('Items must belong to the same farmer.')\n\n    const unitPrice = getTierPrice(item.qty, {\n      priceTier1Qty: listing.price_tier_1_qty,\n      priceTier1Price: listing.price_tier_1_price,\n      priceTier2Qty: listing.price_tier_2_qty,\n      priceTier2Price: listing.price_tier_2_price,\n      priceTier3Price: listing.price_tier_3_price,\n    })\n\n    const linePrice = unitPrice != null ? Math.round(unitPrice * item.qty) : null\n    if (linePrice == null || linePrice <= 0) {\n      return bad(`Price not set for ${listing.name}. Please ask the farmer.`)\n    }\n    total += linePrice\n\n    rows.push({\n      farmer_id: farmerId,\n      produce_listing_id: listing.id,\n      produce_name: listing.name,\n      quantity: item.qty,\n      unit: listing.unit || 'kg',\n      total_price: linePrice,\n      buyer_name: consumer.name || 'Buyer',\n      buyer_phone: consumer.phone,\n      consumer_id: consumer.id,\n      pickup_location: typeof pickupLocation === 'string' ? pickupLocation.slice(0, 200) : null,\n      status: 'pending',\n      payment_method: paymentMethod,\n      payment_status: 'pending',\n      delivery_type: deliveryType,\n      delivery_status: deliveryType === 'home_delivery' ? 'unassigned' : null,\n      delivery_address: deliveryAddress,\n      delivery_landmark: deliveryLandmark,\n      delivery_pincode: deliveryPincode,\n      delivery_alt_phone: deliveryAltPhone,\n      handover_otp: sharedHandoverOtp,\n      // Fee is paid once per cart, so we stamp it on the first row only.\n      // sum(delivery_fee) and sum(rider_payout) over a batch === one fee.\n      delivery_fee: 0,\n      rider_payout: 0,\n    })\n  }\n\n  if (rows.length > 0 && deliveryFee > 0) {\n    rows[0].delivery_fee = deliveryFee\n    rows[0].rider_payout = deliveryFee\n  }\n\n  // Atomic stock claim. decrement_stock returns false if the listing went\n  // below zero (or vanished). On any failure we revert prior claims so we\n  // don't leak inventory.\n  const claimed: Array<{ listingId: string; qty: number }> = []\n  for (const item of items) {\n    const listing = listingById.get(item.listingId)!\n    const { data: ok, error: rpcErr } = await supabase.rpc('decrement_stock', {\n      p_listing_id: item.listingId,\n      p_qty: item.qty,\n    })\n    if (rpcErr) {\n      console.error('[YFF] decrement_stock rpc failed:', rpcErr.message)\n      for (const c of claimed) {\n        await supabase.rpc('increment_stock', { p_listing_id: c.listingId, p_qty: c.qty }).catch(() => null)\n      }\n      return bad('Could not place order. Please try again.', 500)\n    }\n    if (!ok) {\n      for (const c of claimed) {\n        await supabase.rpc('increment_stock', { p_listing_id: c.listingId, p_qty: c.qty }).catch(() => null)\n      }\n      return bad(`${listing.name} just sold out. Please reduce the quantity and try again.`)\n    }\n    claimed.push({ listingId: item.listingId, qty: item.qty })\n  }\n\n  const { data: inserted, error: insertErr } = await supabase\n    .from('orders')\n    .insert(rows)\n    .select('id')\n\n  if (insertErr || !inserted) {\n    console.error('[YFF] place-order insert failed:', insertErr?.message)\n    // Roll the stock back so the row isn't lost.\n    for (const c of claimed) {\n      await supabase.rpc('increment_stock', { p_listing_id: c.listingId, p_qty: c.qty }).catch(() => null)\n    }\n    return bad('Could not place order. Please try again.', 500)\n  }\n\n  return NextResponse.json({\n    ok: true,\n    orderIds: inserted.map((r) => r.id),\n    total,\n    deliveryFee,\n    grandTotal: total + deliveryFee,\n  })\n}"
}

Suspended/pending accounts are kicked\n  // out of /api/rider/me as well, but we double-check here to be safe.\n  const { data: rider } = await supabase\n    .from('delivery_boys')\n    .select('id, status')\n    .eq('id', session.riderId)\n    .maybeSingle()\n\n  if (!rider || rider.status !== 'active') {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Account not active.' }, { status: 403 })\n  }\n\n  // Available — farmer-approved home deliveries that no rider has taken yet.\n  // Mine — anything currently assigned to me and not yet delivered.\n  const { data: availableRaw, error: availErr } = await supabase\n    .from('orders')\n    .select(\n      'id, farmer_id, produce_name, quantity, unit, total_price, payment_method, payment_status, delivery_pincode, delivery_status, created_at',\n    )\n    .eq('delivery_type', 'home_delivery')\n    .eq('status', 'approved')\n    .is('delivery_boy_id', null)\n    .or('delivery_status.is.null,delivery_status.eq.unassigned')\n    .order('created_at', { ascending: true })\n    .limit(50) as { data: OrderRow[] | null; error: { message: string } | null }",
  "new_string": "  // Gate: only active riders see orders. Suspended/pending accounts are kicked\n  // out of /api/rider/me as well, but we double-check here to be safe.\n  const { data: rider } = await supabase\n    .from('delivery_boys')\n    .select('id, status, service_pincodes')\n    .eq('id', session.riderId)\n    .maybeSingle() as { data: { id: string; status: string; service_pincodes: string[] | null } | null }\n\n  if (!rider || rider.status !== 'active') {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Account not active.' }, { status: 403 })\n  }\n\n  // Available — farmer-approved home deliveries that no rider has taken yet.\n  // Mine — anything currently assigned to me and not yet delivered.\n  // If the rider declared service pincodes we only show matching ones,\n  // otherwise (legacy accounts with no pincodes set) we show everything.\n  const ridersPincodes = (rider.service_pincodes ?? []).filter((p) => /^\\d{6}$/.test(p))\n  let availableQuery = supabase\n    .from('orders')\n    .select(\n      'id, farmer_id, produce_name, quantity, unit, total_price, payment_method, payment_status, delivery_pincode, delivery_status, delivery_fee, rider_payout, created_at',\n    )\n    .eq('delivery_type', 'home_delivery')\n    .eq('status', 'approved')\n    .is('delivery_boy_id', null)\n    .or('delivery_status.is.null,delivery_status.eq.unassigned')\n    .order('created_at', { ascending: true })\n    .limit(50)\n  if (ridersPincodes.length > 0) {\n    availableQuery = availableQuery.in('delivery_pincode', ridersPincodes)\n  }\n  const { data: availableRaw, error: availErr } = await availableQuery as\n    { data: OrderRow[] | null; error: { message: string } | null }"
}

Please try again.', 500)\n    }\n    if (!ok) {\n      for (const c of claimed) {\n        await supabase.rpc('increment_stock', { p_listing_id: c.listingId, p_qty: c.qty }).catch(() => null)\n      }\n      return bad(`${listing.name} just sold out. Please reduce the quantity and try again.`)\n    }\n    claimed.push({ listingId: item.listingId, qty: item.qty })\n  }\n\n  const { data: inserted, error: insertErr } = await supabase\n    .from('orders')\n    .insert(rows)\n    .select('id')\n\n  if (insertErr || !inserted) {\n    console.error('[YFF] place-order insert failed:', insertErr?.message)\n    // Roll the stock back so the row isn't lost.\n    for (const c of claimed) {\n      await supabase.rpc('increment_stock', { p_listing_id: c.listingId, p_qty: c.qty }).catch(() => null)\n    }\n    return bad('Could not place order.   "new_string": "  // Atomic stock claim. decrement_stock returns false if the listing went\n  // below zero (or vanished). On any failure we revert prior claims so we\n  // don't leak inventory.\n  const claimed: Array<{ listingId: string; qty: number }> = []\n  const revertClaims = async () => {\n    for (const c of claimed) {\n      try {\n        await supabase.rpc('increment_stock', { p_listing_id: c.listingId, p_qty: c.qty })\n      } catch (e) {\n        console.error('[YFF] increment_stock revert failed:', e)\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  for (const item of items) {\n    const listing = listingById.get(item.listingId)!\n    const { data: ok, error: rpcErr } = await supabase.rpc('decrement_stock', {\n      p_listing_id: item.listingId,\n      p_qty: item.qty,\n    })\n    if (rpcErr) {\n      console.error('[YFF] decrement_stock rpc failed:', rpcErr.message)\n      await revertClaims()\n      return bad('Could not place order. Please try again.', 500)\n    }\n    if (!ok) {\n      await revertClaims()\n      return bad(`${listing.name} just sold out. Please reduce the quantity and try again.`)\n    }\n    claimed.push({ listingId: item.listingId, qty: item.qty })\n  }\n\n  const { data: inserted, error: insertErr } = await supabase\n    .from('orders')\n    .insert(rows)\n    .select('id')\n\n  if (insertErr || !inserted) {\n    console.error('[YFF] place-order insert failed:', insertErr?.message)\n    // Roll the stock back so the row isn't lost.\n    await revertClaims()\n    return bad('Could not place order. Please try again.', 500)\n  }"
}

> AGENT

Typecheck is clean. Here's the bundle:

**What's done**

1. **No more overselling** — `scripts/must-fix-migration.sql` adds atomic `decrement_stock` / `increment_stock` RPCs. `src/app/api/orders/place/route.ts` calls them per item before inserting the order; if any item can't be claimed, prior claims are reverted and the order fails clean. If the order insert itself fails, stock is also reverted. The farmer dashboard's decline action restocks too (`src/app/farmer/dashboard/page.tsx:314-334`).

2. **Riders only see deliveries in their pincodes** — new `service_pincodes text[]` column on `delivery_boys`. Captured as a 6-digit comma list at rider signup (`src/app/rider/signup/page.tsx`), parsed/validated in `src/app/api/rider/register/route.ts`, and used to filter the rider's Available feed in `src/app/api/rider/orders/route.ts`. Legacy riders with no pincodes set still see everything, so nothing breaks.

3. **Delivery fee plumbing in place, currently free** — per your latest message, `DELIVERY_FEE_RUPEES = 0` in `src/lib/delivery-fee.ts`. The cart shows the old single-line "Estimated total" and the rider earning badge/COD upcharge stay hidden whenever the fee is 0 or `rider_payout` is 0. To turn fees on later, just edit that one constant. The DB columns (`delivery_fee`, `rider_payout`) are added by the same migration so they're ready when you flip the switch.

**Action required on your side**

Run `scripts/must-fix-migration.sql` in Supabase SQL Editor (it's idempotent — safe to re-run).

**End-of-turn:** Stock loophole closed, rider pincode routing live, delivery fee infrastructure parked at ₹0 ready to enable later. Next would be order status notifications (#4) when you're ready.

> AGENT

Done. Typecheck clean.

**What the rider now sees**

A new **History / చరిత్ర** tab next to Available and My Deliveries, listing every order they've delivered (most recent first, up to 100). Each entry shows the produce, farmer, buyer pincode, delivery time, and — when the delivery fee is on — the rupees earned for that drop.

Above the list is a small summary strip: total completed deliveries and total earned (₹0 right now since `DELIVERY_FEE_RUPEES = 0`, but it'll start populating the moment you flip that constant).

**Files touched**
- `src/app/api/rider/orders/route.ts` — new history + totalEarned in the response (uses `delivered_at` for ordering, scoped to `delivery_status='delivered'` and `delivery_boy_id=rider`).
- `src/app/rider/dashboard/page.tsx` — third tab, summary strip, new `HistoryCard` component.

No DB migration needed for this — `delivered_at` already exists from `delivery-feature-migration.sql`.
